02352: /* =========================
02353:    Motor principal V2 (FOCO = BICHO)
02354: ========================= */
02355: 
02356: export function computeConditionalNextTop3V2({
02357:   lotteryKey,
02358:   drawsRange,
02359:   drawLast,
02360:   drawsToday,
02361:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02362:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02363:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02364:   topN = 3,
02365:   targetYmdOverride = "",
02366:   targetHourOverride = "",
02367: }) {
02368:   const list = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
02369:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) {
02370:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02371:   }
02372: 
02373:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
02374: 
02375:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
02376:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
02377:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
02378: 
02379:   if (
02380:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
02381:     !lastH ||
02382:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
02383:     Number(prevGrupo) <= 0
02384:   ) {
02385:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02386:   }
02387: 
02388:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
02389:     lotteryKey,
02390:     ymd: lastY,
02391:     hourBucket: lastH,
02392:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02393:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02394:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02395:   });
02396: 
02397:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
02398:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
02399: 
02400:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02401:     ? forcedTargetY
02402:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
02403: 
02404:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
02405:     ? forcedTargetH
02406:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
02407:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
02408:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
02409: 
02410:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
02411:     return { top: [], meta: null };
02412:   }
02413: 
02414:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
02415: 
02416:   const dayContext = buildDayContext(drawsToday);
02417:   const prevPair =
02418:     Array.isArray(dayContext?.seq) && dayContext.seq.length >= 2
02419:       ? dayContext.seq.slice(-2)
02420:       : [];
02421: 
02422:   const pairOut = buildPairSequenceDistribution({
02423:     drawsRange: list,
02424:     lotteryKey: key,
02425:     targetHour: targetH,
02426:     targetDow,
02427:     targetDayOfMonth,
02428:     prevPair,
02429:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02430:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02431:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02432:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02433:   });
02434: 
02435:   const pairConfidence = clamp01(
02436:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.samples || 0, 6) * 0.7) +
02437:     (confidenceFromSamples(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0, 3) * 0.3)
02438:   );
02439: 
02440:   const layerOut = buildConditionalLayerDistribution({
02441:     lotteryKey,
02442:     drawsRange: list,
02443:     targetHour: targetH,
02444:     targetDow,
02445:     targetDayOfMonth,
02446:     prevHour: lastH,
02447:     prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02448:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02449:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02450:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02451:   });
02452: 
02453:   const chosen = layerOut?.chosen || null;
02454:   const condSamples = Number(chosen?.samples || 0);
02455:   const condFirstFreq = chosen?.firstFreq || new Map();
02456: 
02457:   const pTransitionFirst = new Map();
02458:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) {
02459:     pTransitionFirst.set(
02460:       g,
02461:       Number(condFirstFreq.get(g) || 0) / Math.max(1, condSamples)
02462:     );
02463:   }
02464: 
02465:   const structuralFirst = computeStructuralFirstDistribution(
02466:     list,
02467:     key,
02468:     targetH,
02469:     targetDow,
02470:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02471:   );
02472:   const pStructuralFirst = structuralFirst?.prob || new Map();
02473: 
02474:   const structuralPrizePresence = computeComparablePrizePresenceDistribution(
02475:     list,
02476:     key,
02477:     targetH,
02478:     targetDow,
02479:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02480:   );
02481:   const pStructuralPrizePresence = structuralPrizePresence?.prob || new Map();
02482: 
02483:   const duplication = computeComparableDuplicationDistribution(
02484:     list,
02485:     key,
02486:     targetH,
02487:     targetDow,
02488:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02489:   );
02490:   const pDuplication = duplication?.prob || new Map();
02491: 
02492:   const comparableSeq = buildComparableFirstSequence(
02493:     list,
02494:     key,
02495:     targetH,
02496:     targetDow
02497:   );
02498: 
02499:   const currentState = getStateFromComparableSequenceBeforeTs(
02500:     comparableSeq,
02501:     targetTs,
02502:     3
02503:   );
02504: 
02505:   const memoryOut = buildMemoryStateDistribution({
02506:     drawsRange: list,
02507:     lotteryKey: key,
02508:     targetHour: targetH,
02509:     targetDow,
02510:     currentState,
02511:     groupsK: TOP3_GROUPS_K,
02512:   });
02513: 
02514:   const pMemory = memoryOut?.prob || new Map();
02515: 
02516:   const seq2Out = buildSeq2FirstDistribution(
02517:     comparableSeq,
02518:     currentState,
02519:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02520:   );
02521:   const pSeq2 = seq2Out?.prob || new Map();
02522: 
02523:   const repeatBoostOut = buildRecentRepeatBoostMap(
02524:     comparableSeq,
02525:     targetTs,
02526:     4,
02527:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02528:   );
02529:   const repeatBoostMap = repeatBoostOut?.boost || new Map();
02530: 
02531:   const regimeInfo = detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(
02532:     getComparableRecentDrawsBeforeTs(comparableSeq, targetTs, 6)
02533:   );
02534: 
02535:   const regime = safeStr(regimeInfo?.regime || "neutral");
02536: 
02537:   const mode =
02538:     dayContext.repeatNow || Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5
02539:       ? "REPEAT"
02540:       : Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75
02541:         ? "SPREAD"
02542:         : "NEUTRAL";
02543: 
02544:   const weights = getDayDrivenWeights(mode);
02545: 
02546:   const recentContextDraws = getComparableContextDrawsBeforeTs(
02547:     list,
02548:     key,
02549:     targetH,
02550:     targetDow,
02551:     targetTs,
02552:     8
02553:   );
02554:   const recentMetrics = computeRecentBichoMetrics(recentContextDraws, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
02555: 
02556:   const recentMaxPrizePresence = Math.max(
02557:     1,
02558:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentPrizePresence || 0))
02559:   );
02560:   const recentMaxFirst = Math.max(
02561:     1,
02562:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentFirst || 0))
02563:   );
02564:   const recentMaxDup = Math.max(
02565:     1,
02566:     ...Array.from(recentMetrics.values()).map((x) => Number(x.recentDupDraws || 0))
02567:   );
02568: 
02569:   const lateSeen = computeComparableLastSeenByGrupo(
02570:     list,
02571:     key,
02572:     targetH,
02573:     targetDow,
02574:     TOP3_GROUPS_K
02575:   );
02576: 
02577:   const finiteSeen = Array.from(lateSeen.values()).filter((x) =>
02578:     Number.isFinite(Number(x))
02579:   );
02580:   const minSeenTs = finiteSeen.length ? Math.min(...finiteSeen) : targetTs;
02581:   const maxGapMsBase =
02582:     Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(minSeenTs)
02583:       ? Math.max(1, targetTs - minSeenTs)
02584:       : 1;
02585: 
02586:   const dayFlowConfidence = confidenceFromSamples(dayContext?.total || 0, 4);
02587:   const pairStrong = Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) >= 4;
02588: 
02589:   const confidence =
02590:     (pairConfidence * 0.3) +
02591:     (confidenceFromSamples(condSamples, 20) * 0.4) +
02592:     (confidenceFromSamples(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0, 15) * 0.3);
02593: 
02594:   const adjustedWeights = normalizeWeights({
02595:     transition: pairStrong ? weights.transition * 0.7 : weights.transition,
02596:     pair: pairStrong ? weights.pair * 1.4 : weights.pair,
02597:     memory: weights.memory,
02598:     recent: weights.recent,
02599:     structural: weights.structural,
02600:     late: weights.late,
02601:   });
02602: 
02603:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
02604:     const grupo = idx + 1;
02605: 
02606:     const pT = Number(pTransitionFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
02607:     const pSF = Number(pStructuralFirst.get(grupo) || 0);
02608:     const pST = Number(pStructuralPrizePresence.get(grupo) || 0);
02609:     const pM = Number(pMemory.get(grupo) || 0);
02610:     const pD = Number(pDuplication.get(grupo) || 0);
02611:     const pSeq = Number(pSeq2.get(grupo) || 0);
02612:     const repeatBoost = Number(repeatBoostMap.get(grupo) || 0);
02613:     const pPair = Number(pairOut?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02614: 
02615:     const rm = recentMetrics.get(grupo) || {
02616:       recentPrizePresence: 0,
02617:       recentFirst: 0,
02618:       recentDupDraws: 0,
02619:       recentLast1PrizePresence: 0,
02620:       recentLast2PrizePresence: 0,
02621:       recentLast3PrizePresence: 0,
02622:     };
02623: 
02624:     const recentPrizePresenceNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentPrizePresence, recentMaxPrizePresence);
02625:     const recentFirstNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentFirst, recentMaxFirst);
02626:     const recentDupNorm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentDupDraws, recentMaxDup);
02627:     const recentLast1Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast1PrizePresence, 1);
02628:     const recentLast2Norm = normalizeMetric(rm.recentLast2PrizePresence, 2);
02629: 
02630:     const recentComposite =
02631:       (recentFirstNorm * 0.52) +
02632:       (recentPrizePresenceNorm * 0.08) +
02633:       (recentDupNorm * 0.18) +
02634:       (recentLast2Norm * 0.14) +
02635:       (recentLast1Norm * 0.1);
02636: 
02637:     const dominanceScore =
02638:       (pT * 0.42) +
02639:       (pSF * 0.26) +
02640:       (pST * 0.18) +
02641:       (pD * 0.14);
02642: 
02643:     const seenTs = Number(lateSeen.get(grupo));
02644:     const gapMs =
02645:       Number.isFinite(targetTs) && Number.isFinite(seenTs)
02646:         ? Math.max(0, targetTs - seenTs)
02647:         : maxGapMsBase;
02648: 
02649:     const lateNorm = Math.max(
02650:       0,
02651:       Math.min(1, gapMs / Math.max(1, maxGapMsBase))
02652:     );
02653: 
02654:     const dayFreq = Number(dayContext?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02655:     const dayFirstFreq = Number(dayContext?.firstFreq?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
02656:     const isDominantToday =
02657:       Number.isFinite(Number(dayContext?.dominante)) &&
02658:       Number(dayContext.dominante) === Number(grupo);
02659: 
02660:     let dayFlowRaw = 0;
02661: 
02662:     if (dayFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 32;
02663:     if (dayFreq >= 3) dayFlowRaw += 18;
02664:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 1) dayFlowRaw += 20;
02665:     if (dayFirstFreq >= 2) dayFlowRaw += 14;
02666:     if (isDominantToday) dayFlowRaw += 24;
02667: 
02668:     if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) < 0.5) {
02669:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq > 0 ? 12 : -8;
02670:     } else if (Number(dayContext?.diversidade || 0) > 0.75) {
02671:       dayFlowRaw += dayFreq === 0 ? 10 : 0;
02672:     }
02673: 
02674:     if (dayContext?.repeatNow && dayFreq > 0) {
02675:       dayFlowRaw += 10;
02676:     }
02677: 
02678:     const dayFlowScore = dayFlowRaw * dayFlowConfidence;
02679: 
02680:     const pairSequenceScore =
02681:       Number(pairOut?.samples || 0) > 0
02682:         ? ((pPair * 260) + Math.min(70, Number(pairOut.samples || 0) * 8)) * pairConfidence
02683:         : 0;
02684: 
02685:     const structuralBlend =
02686:       (pSF * 0.45) +
02687:       (pST * 0.35) +
02688:       (pD * 0.2);
02689: 
02690:     const memoryBlend =
02691:       (pM * 0.75) +
02692:       (pSeq * 0.25);
02693: 
02694:     const recentBlend =
02695:       (recentComposite * 0.85) +
02696:       (repeatBoost * 0.15);
02697: 
02698:     const prob =
02699:       (pT * adjustedWeights.transition) +
02700:       (pPair * adjustedWeights.pair * pairConfidence) +
02701:       (memoryBlend * adjustedWeights.memory) +
02702:       (recentBlend * adjustedWeights.recent) +
02703:       (structuralBlend * adjustedWeights.structural) +
02704:       (lateNorm * adjustedWeights.late);
02705: 
02706:     const score =
02707:       (prob * 1000) +
02708:       (dominanceScore * 180) +
02709:       (pairSequenceScore * 0.6) +
02710:       (dayFlowScore * 0.8) +
02711:       (confidence * 50);
02712: 
02713:     return {
02714:       grupo,
02715:       prob,
02716:       scoreProb: prob,
02717:       score,
02718:       probTransition: pT,
02719:       probStructuralFirst: pSF,
02720:       probStructuralPrizePresence: pST,
02721:       probMemory: pM,
02722:       probDuplication: pD,
02723:       probSeq2: pSeq,
02724:       probPair: pPair,
02725:       pairProb: pPair,
02726:       pairConfidence,
02727:       repeatBoost,
02728:       dominanceScore,
02729:       recentComposite,
02730:       recentPrizePresence: rm.recentPrizePresence,
02731:       recentFirst: rm.recentFirst,
02732:       recentDupDraws: rm.recentDupDraws,
02733:       recentLast1PrizePresence: rm.recentLast1PrizePresence,
02734:       recentLast2PrizePresence: rm.recentLast2PrizePresence,
02735:       recentLast3PrizePresence: rm.recentLast3PrizePresence,
02736:       lateNorm,
02737:       gapMs,
02738:       condFirstCount: Number(condFirstFreq.get(grupo) || 0),
02739:       structuralFirstCount: Number(structuralFirst?.firstFreq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02740:       structuralPrizePresenceCount: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02741:       duplicationCount: Number(duplication?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02742:       memoryWeight: Number(memoryOut?.freq?.get(grupo) || 0),
02743:       dayFreq,
02744:       dayFirstFreq,
02745:       isDominantToday,
02746:       dayFlowConfidence,
02747:       dayFlowScore,
02748:       pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
02749:       pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
02750:       pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
02751:       pairCount: Number(pairOut?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
02752:       pairSequenceScore,
02753:     };
02754:   })
02755:     .sort((a, b) => {
02756:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
02757:       if (b.scoreProb !== a.scoreProb) return b.scoreProb - a.scoreProb;
02758:       if (b.dominanceScore !== a.dominanceScore) return b.dominanceScore - a.dominanceScore;
02759:       if (b.probTransition !== a.probTransition) return b.probTransition - a.probTransition;
02760:       if (b.probPair !== a.probPair) return b.probPair - a.probPair;
02761:       if (b.probDuplication !== a.probDuplication) return b.probDuplication - a.probDuplication;
02762:       if (b.probStructuralFirst !== a.probStructuralFirst) return b.probStructuralFirst - a.probStructuralFirst;
02763:       if (b.probStructuralPrizePresence !== a.probStructuralPrizePresence) return b.probStructuralPrizePresence - a.probStructuralPrizePresence;
02764:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
02765:     })
02766:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
02767: 
02768: 
02769: 
02770:   const reasonsBase = [
02771:     `Motor: V2_BICHO`,
02772:     `Regime detectado: ${regime}`,
02773:     `RepeatRate=${Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0).toFixed(2)} | UniqueRate=${Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
02774:     `Pesos: mode=${mode} | transição=${(adjustedWeights.transition * 100).toFixed(0)}% | par=${(adjustedWeights.pair * 100).toFixed(0)}% | memória=${(adjustedWeights.memory * 100).toFixed(0)}% | recência=${(adjustedWeights.recent * 100).toFixed(0)}% | estrutural=${(adjustedWeights.structural * 100).toFixed(0)}% | atraso=${(adjustedWeights.late * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
02775:     `Estado atual: prev=${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → alvo ${targetH}`,
02776:     `Estado curto comparável: [${currentState.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join(", ")}]`,
02777:     `Camada condicional: ${chosen?.label || "—"} | amostras=${condSamples}`,
02778:     `Amostra estrutural 1º=${Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0)} | PRIZE_PRESENCE=${Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0)} | duplicação=${Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0)}`,
02779:     `Amostra memória: matches=${Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0)} | peso=${Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
02780:     `Par do dia: ${Array.isArray(prevPair) && prevPair.length === 2 ? prevPair.map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0")).join("→") : "--"} | matches=${Number(pairOut?.samples || 0)} | matches DOM=${Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0)} | confiança=${(pairConfidence * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
02781:     `Fluxo do dia: draws=${Number(dayContext?.total || 0)} | confiança=${(dayFlowConfidence * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
02782:     `Data alvo: ${targetY} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
02783:   ];
02784: 
02785:   const top = ranked.map((x, idx) => {
02786:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
02787: 
02788:     return {
02789:       rank: idx + 1,
02790:       title:
02791:         idx === 0
02792:           ? "Mais provável"
02793:           : idx === 1
02794:             ? "2º mais provável"
02795:             : "3º mais provável",
02796:       grupo: x.grupo,
02797:       scoreProb: Number(x.scoreProb || 0),
02798:       probCond: Number(x.probTransition || 0),
02799:       probBase: Number(x.probStructuralFirst || 0),
02800:       lateBonus: Number(x.lateNorm || 0),
02801:       freq: x.condFirstCount,
02802:       freqCond: x.condFirstCount,
02803:       freqBase: x.structuralFirstCount,
02804:       freqZeroWhy:
02805:         x.condFirstCount <= 0
02806:           ? `Sem 1º lugar nesta camada (${chosen?.label || "—"}).`
02807:           : "",
02808:       reasons: [
02809:         ...reasonsBase,
02810:         `Grupo G${g2}: transição para 1º=${(x.probTransition * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02811:         `Grupo G${g2}: estrutural de 1º=${(x.probStructuralFirst * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02812:         `Grupo G${g2}: estrutural de TOP3=${(x.probStructuralPrizePresence * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02813:         `Grupo G${g2}: duplicação histórica=${(x.probDuplication * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02814:         `Grupo G${g2}: sequência ordem 2=${(x.probSeq2 * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02815:         `Grupo G${g2}: par do dia=${(x.probPair * 100).toFixed(2)}% | confiança=${(x.pairConfidence * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02816:         `Grupo G${g2}: boost de repetição=${(x.repeatBoost * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02817:         `Grupo G${g2}: memória curta=${(x.probMemory * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02818:         `Grupo G${g2}: recência composta=${(x.recentComposite * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02819:         `Grupo G${g2}: atraso normalizado=${(x.lateNorm * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02820:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs na camada=${x.condFirstCount}`,
02821:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs estruturais=${x.structuralFirstCount}`,
02822:         `Grupo G${g2}: TOP3 estruturais=${x.structuralPrizePresenceCount}`,
02823:         `Grupo G${g2}: draws com duplicação=${x.duplicationCount}`,
02824:         `Grupo G${g2}: peso de memória=${Number(x.memoryWeight || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
02825:         `Grupo G${g2}: frequência no dia=${x.dayFreq}`,
02826:         `Grupo G${g2}: 1ºs no dia=${x.dayFirstFreq}`,
02827:         `Grupo G${g2}: dominante no dia=${x.isDominantToday ? "sim" : "não"}`,
02828:         `Grupo G${g2}: score do fluxo do dia=${Number(x.dayFlowScore || 0).toFixed(2)} | confiança=${(Number(x.dayFlowConfidence || 0) * 100).toFixed(0)}%`,
02829:         `Grupo G${g2}: sequência ${Array.isArray(prevPair) && prevPair.length === 2 ? prevPair.map((n) => String(n).padStart(2, "0")).join("→") : "--"} | amostras=${x.pairSamples} | amostras ponderadas=${Number(x.pairWeightedSamples || 0).toFixed(2)} | DOM exato=${x.pairExactDomSamples} | ocorrências=${Number(x.pairCount || 0).toFixed(2)} | prob=${(Number(x.pairProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}% | score=${Number(x.pairSequenceScore || 0).toFixed(2)}`,
02830:         `Probabilidade final BICHO=${(x.scoreProb * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
02831:       ],
02832:       meta: {
02833:         trigger: {
02834:           ymd: lastY,
02835:           hour: lastH,
02836:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02837:         },
02838:         next: {
02839:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
02840:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
02841:         },
02842:         samples: condSamples,
02843:         scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
02844:         explain: {
02845:           engine: "V2_BICHO",
02846:           mode,
02847:           regime,
02848:           repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
02849:           uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
02850:           weights: adjustedWeights,
02851:           layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
02852:           layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",

02856:           duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
02857:           memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),

02861:           seq2Prev1: Number(seq2Out?.prev1 || 0),
02862:           pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
02863:           pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
02864:           pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
02865:           pairConfidence,
02866:           repeatWindowSize: Number(repeatBoostOut?.windowSize || 0),
02867:           repeatWindowConsidered: Number(repeatBoostOut?.considered || 0),
02868:           currentState,
02869:           targetDow,
02870:           targetDayOfMonth,
02871:           prevHour: lastH,
02872:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02873:           dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
02874:           dayFlowConfidence,
02875:           dominanceScore: x.dominanceScore,
02876:           recentComposite: x.recentComposite,
02877:           recentPrizePresence: x.recentPrizePresence,
02878:           recentFirst: x.recentFirst,
02879:           recentDupDraws: x.recentDupDraws,
02880:           allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
02881:             key: layer.key,
02882:             label: layer.label,
02883:             samples: layer.samples,
02884:             minSamples: layer.minSamples,
02885:           })),
02886:         },
02887:       },
02888:     };
02889:   });
02890: 
02891:   return {
02892:     top,
02893:     meta: {
02894:       trigger: {
02895:         ymd: lastY,
02896:         hour: lastH,
02897:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02898:       },
02899:       next: {
02900:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
02901:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
02902:       },
02903:       samples: condSamples,
02904:       scenario: `V2_BICHO_${safeStr(regime).toUpperCase()}`,
02905:       explain: {
02906:         engine: "V2_BICHO",
02907:         mode,
02908:         regime,
02909:         repeatRate: Number(regimeInfo?.repeatRate || 0),
02910:         uniqueRate: Number(regimeInfo?.uniqueRate || 0),
02911:         weights: adjustedWeights,
02912:         layerKey: chosen?.key || "NONE",
02913:         layerLabel: chosen?.label || "—",
02914:         layerSamples: condSamples,
02915:         structuralFirstSamples: Number(structuralFirst?.totalSamples || 0),
02916:         structuralPrizePresenceSamples: Number(structuralPrizePresence?.totalSamples || 0),
02917:         duplicationSamples: Number(duplication?.totalSamples || 0),
02918:         memoryMatchedSamples: Number(memoryOut?.matchedSamples || 0),
02919:         memoryTotalWeight: Number(memoryOut?.totalWeight || 0),
02920:         pairSamples: Number(pairOut?.samples || 0),
02921:         pairWeightedSamples: Number(pairOut?.weightedSamples || 0),
02922:         pairExactDomSamples: Number(pairOut?.exactDomSamples || 0),
02923:         pairConfidence,
02924:         currentState,
02925:         targetDow,
02926:         targetDayOfMonth,
02927:         prevHour: lastH,
02928:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
02929:         dayContextTotal: Number(dayContext?.total || 0),
02930:         dayFlowConfidence,
02931:         allLayers: (layerOut?.layers || []).map((layer) => ({
02932:           key: layer.key,
02933:           label: layer.label,
02934:           samples: layer.samples,
02935:           minSamples: layer.minSamples,
02936:         })),
02937:       },
02938:     },
02939:   };
02940: }
02941: 
02942: 
02943: /* =========================
02944:    Motor principal V3 (ESTATISTICO AUDITAVEL)
02945: ========================= */
02946: 
02947: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
02948:   const k = Number(key);
02949:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
02950:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
02951: }
02952: 
02953: function emptyGroupMap() {
02954:   const out = new Map();
02955:   for (let g = 1; g <= safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25); g += 1) out.set(g, 0);
02956:   return out;
02957: }
02958: 
02959: function getPrizePresenceGroupSet(draw) {
02960:   return new Set(
02961:     getAllPrizePresenceGroups(draw)
02962:       .map((x) => Number(x.grupo))
02963:       .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= TOP3_GROUPS_K)
02964:   );
02965: }
02966: 
02967: function layerProbability(freqMap, samples, alpha = TOP3_SMOOTH_ALPHA) {
02968:   const out = new Map();
02969:   const k = safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25);
02970:   const a = Math.max(0, Number(alpha || 0));
02971:   const base = Math.max(0, Number(samples || 0));
02972:   const denom = Math.max(1, base + (a * k));
02973: 
02974:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
02975:     const count = Math.max(0, Number(freqMap?.get?.(g) || 0));
02976:     out.set(g, (count + a) / denom);
02977:   }
02978: 
02979:   return out;
02980: }
02981: 
02982: function sampleConfidence(samples, fullAt = 30) {
02983:   const n = Number(samples || 0);
02984:   const lim = Math.max(1, Number(fullAt || 30));
02985:   return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n / lim));
02986: }
02987: 
02988: export function computeStatisticalTop3V3({
02989:   lotteryKey,
02990:   drawsRange,
02991:   drawLast,
02992:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
02993:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
02994:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
02995:   topN = 3,
02996:   targetYmdOverride = "",
02997:   targetHourOverride = "",
02998:   drawsAlreadySorted = false,
02999: }) {
03000:   const sourceList =
03001:     Array.isArray(drawsRange)
03002:       ? drawsRange
03003:       : [];
03004: 
03005:   const list = drawsAlreadySorted
03006:     ? sourceList
03007:     : sortDrawsAsc(sourceList);
03008:   if (!list.length || !drawLast) return { top: [], meta: null };
03009: 
03010:   const key = safeStr(lotteryKey).toUpperCase();
03011: 
03012:   const lastY = pickDrawYMD(drawLast);
03013:   const lastH = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(drawLast));
03014:   const prevGrupo = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(drawLast);
03015: 
03016:   if (
03017:     !isYMD(lastY) ||
03018:     !lastH ||
03019:     !Number.isFinite(Number(prevGrupo)) ||
03020:     Number(prevGrupo) < 1 ||
03021:     Number(prevGrupo) > TOP3_GROUPS_K
03022:   ) {
03023:     return { top: [], meta: null };
03024:   }
03025: 
03026:   const nextSlot = getNextSlotForLottery({
03027:     lotteryKey: key,
03028:     ymd: lastY,
03029:     hourBucket: lastH,
03030:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03031:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03032:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03033:   });
03034: 
03035:   const forcedTargetY = safeStr(targetYmdOverride);
03036:   const forcedTargetH = toHourBucket(targetHourOverride);
03037: 
03038:   const targetY = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
03039:     ? forcedTargetY
03040:     : safeStr(nextSlot?.ymd);
03041: 
03042:   const targetH = isYMD(forcedTargetY) && forcedTargetH
03043:     ? forcedTargetH
03044:     : toHourBucket(nextSlot?.hour);
03045:   const targetDow = getDowKey(targetY);
03046:   const targetDayOfMonth = getDayOfMonth(targetY);
03047:   const targetTs = ymdHourToTs(targetY, targetH);
03048: 
03049:   if (!isYMD(targetY) || !targetH || !Number.isFinite(Number(targetDow))) {
03050:     return { top: [], meta: null };
03051:   }
03052: 
03053:   const history = list.filter((d) => {
03054:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03055:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03056:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
03057:     return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < targetTs;
03058:   });
03059: 
03060:   const layers = {
03061:     hour: { label: `frequência no horário ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.33 },
03062:     dowHour: { label: `frequência no dia da semana + horário`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.27 },
03063:     dayMonth: { label: `frequência no dia ${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.04 },
03064:     transition: { label: `transição G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH} → ${targetH}`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.26 },
03065:     recent: { label: `recência comparável`, samples: 0, first: emptyGroupMap(), prizePresence: emptyGroupMap(), weight: 0.10 },
03066:   };
03067: 
03068:   for (const d of history) {
03069:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03070:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03071:     const dow = getDowKey(y);
03072:     const dom = getDayOfMonth(y);
03073:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
03074:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
03075: 
03076:     const addToLayer = (layer) => {
03077:       layer.samples += 1;
03078:       incMap(layer.first, g1, 1);
03079:       for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layer.prizePresence, g, 1);
03080:     };
03081: 
03082:     if (h === targetH) addToLayer(layers.hour);
03083:     if (h === targetH && Number(dow) === Number(targetDow)) addToLayer(layers.dowHour);
03084:     if (Number(dom) === Number(targetDayOfMonth)) addToLayer(layers.dayMonth);
03085:   }
03086: 
03087:   const indexed = indexDrawsByYmdHour(history);
03088: 
03089:   for (const prev of history) {
03090:     const py = pickDrawYMD(prev);
03091:     const ph = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(prev));
03092:     const pg = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(prev);
03093: 
03094:     if (!isYMD(py) || !ph || Number(pg) !== Number(prevGrupo)) continue;
03095: 
03096:     const ns = getNextSlotForLottery({
03097:       lotteryKey: key,
03098:       ymd: py,
03099:       hourBucket: ph,
03100:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
03101:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
03102:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
03103:     });
03104: 
03105:     if (toHourBucket(ns?.hour) !== targetH) continue;
03106:     if (ph !== lastH) continue;
03107: 
03108:     const nextDraw = indexed.get(`${safeStr(ns?.ymd)}|${toHourBucket(ns?.hour)}`) || null;
03109:     if (!nextDraw) continue;
03110: 
03111:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(nextDraw);
03112:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(nextDraw);
03113: 
03114:     layers.transition.samples += 1;
03115:     incMap(layers.transition.first, g1, 1);
03116:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.transition.prizePresence, g, 1);
03117:   }
03118: 
03119:   const recentComparable = history
03120:     .filter((d) => {
03121:       const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03122:       const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03123:       return h === targetH && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
03124:     })
03125:     .slice(-20);
03126: 
03127:   for (const d of recentComparable) {
03128:     const g1 = getFirstGrupoFromDraw(d);
03129:     const prizePresenceSet = getPrizePresenceGroupSet(d);
03130: 
03131:     layers.recent.samples += 1;
03132:     incMap(layers.recent.first, g1, 1);
03133:     for (const g of prizePresenceSet) incMap(layers.recent.prizePresence, g, 1);
03134:   }
03135: 
03136:   const currentScene = buildSceneFromDraw(drawLast);
03137:   const sceneRanking = buildHistoricalSceneRanking(history, currentScene, 80);
03138:   const sceneHypothesis = buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, TOP3_GROUPS_K);
03139:   const sceneWeight =
03140:     sampleConfidence(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0, TOP3_SCENE_SAMPLE_TARGET) *
03141:     TOP3_SCENE_WEIGHT;
03142: 
03143: 
03144:   const activeWeights = {};
03145:   let totalWeight = 0;
03146: 
03147:   for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
03148:     const conf =
03149:       keyLayer === "transition"
03150:         ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 8)
03151:         : keyLayer === "recent"
03152:           ? sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 12)
03153:           : sampleConfidence(layer.samples, 30);
03154: 
03155:     const w = layer.samples > 0 ? layer.weight * conf : 0;
03156:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = w;
03157:     totalWeight += w;
03158:   }
03159: 
03160:   if (totalWeight <= 0) {
03161:     activeWeights.hour = 1;
03162:     totalWeight = 1;
03163:   }
03164: 
03165:   for (const keyLayer of Object.keys(activeWeights)) {
03166:     activeWeights[keyLayer] = activeWeights[keyLayer] / totalWeight;
03167:   }
03168: 
03169:   const layerProbabilities = Object.fromEntries(
03170:     Object.entries(layers).map(([keyLayer, layer]) => [
03171:       keyLayer,
03172:       {
03173:         first: layerProbability(layer.first, layer.samples),
03174:         prizePresence: layerProbability(layer.prizePresence, layer.samples),
03175:       },
03176:     ])
03177:   );
03178: 
03179:   const ranked = Array.from({ length: safeInt(TOP3_GROUPS_K, 25) }, (_, idx) => {
03180:     const grupo = idx + 1;
03181: 
03182:     let scoreProb = 0;
03183:     const details = {};
03184: 
03185:     for (const [keyLayer, layer] of Object.entries(layers)) {
03186:       const probabilities = layerProbabilities[keyLayer];
03187: 
03188:       const pFirst = Number(probabilities.first.get(grupo) || 0);
03189:       const pPrizePresence = Number(probabilities.prizePresence.get(grupo) || 0);
03190: 
03191:       const pLayer = (pFirst * 0.92) + (pPrizePresence * 0.08);
03192:       const w = Number(activeWeights[keyLayer] || 0);
03193: 
03194:       scoreProb += pLayer * w;
03195: 
03196:       details[keyLayer] = {
03197:         label: layer.label,
03198:         samples: layer.samples,
03199:         firstCount: Number(layer.first.get(grupo) || 0),
03200:         top3Count: Number(layer.prizePresence.get(grupo) || 0),
03201:         probability: pLayer,
03202:         weight: w,
03203:       };
03204:     }
03205: 
03206:     const pSceneRaw = Number(sceneHypothesis?.prob?.get?.(grupo) || 0);
03207:     const pScene =
03208:       (pSceneRaw * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_SCENE) +
03209:       ((1 / TOP3_GROUPS_K) * TOP3_SCENE_BLEND_UNIFORM);
03210: 
03211:     if (sceneWeight > 0) {
03212:       scoreProb = (scoreProb * (1 - sceneWeight)) + (pScene * sceneWeight);
03213:     }
03214: 
03215:     details.scene = {
03216:       label: "analogia histórica de cena",
03217:       samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
03218:       firstCount: Number(sceneHypothesis?.freq?.get?.(grupo) || 0),
03219:       top3Count: 0,
03220:       probability: pScene,
03221:       weight: sceneWeight,
03222:     };
03223: 
03224:     return {
03225:       grupo,
03226:       scoreProb,
03227:       score: scoreProb * 1000,
03228:       details,
03229:     };
03230:   })
03231:     .sort((a, b) => {
03232:       if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
03233:       return a.grupo - b.grupo;
03234:     })
03235:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(topN || 3)));
03236: 
03237:   const rankedScored = scoreRanking(
03238:     ranked,
03239:     {
03240:       lotteryKey,
03241:       targetYmd: targetY,
03242:       targetHour: targetH,
03243:       previousGroup: prevGrupo,
03244:       totalDraws: history.length,
03245:     }
03246:   );
03247: 
03248:   const top = rankedScored.map((x, idx) => {
03249:     const g2 = String(x.grupo).padStart(2, "0");
03250:     const rawScoreProb = Number(x.scoreProb || 0);
03251: 
03252:     const strongest = Object.values(x.details)
03253:       .filter((d) => Number(d.probability || 0) > 0)
03254:       .sort((a, b) => (b.probability * b.weight) - (a.probability * a.weight))
03255:       .slice(0, 3);
03256: 
03257:     return {
03258:       rank: idx + 1,
03259:       title:
03260:         idx === 0
03261:           ? "Mais provável"
03262:           : idx === 1
03263:             ? "2º mais provável"
03264:             : "3º mais provável",
03265:       grupo: x.grupo,
03266:       scoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
03267:       rawScoreProb: Number(rawScoreProb || 0),
03268:       probCond: Number(x.details.transition?.probability || 0),
03269:       probBase: Number(x.details.hour?.probability || 0),
03270:       lateBonus: 0,
03271:       freq: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
03272:       freqCond: Number(x.details.transition?.firstCount || 0),
03273:       freqBase: Number(x.details.hour?.firstCount || 0),
03274:       freqZeroWhy: "",
03275:       reasons: [
03276:         `Motor: V3_STATISTICAL`,
03277:         `Alvo: ${targetY} ${targetH} | DOW=${targetDow} | dia=${String(targetDayOfMonth).padStart(2, "0")}`,
03278:         `Base: G${String(prevGrupo).padStart(2, "0")} @ ${lastH}`,
03279:         `Probabilidade final G${g2}: ${(Number(rawScoreProb || 0) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`,
03280:         ...strongest.map((d) =>
03281:           `${d.label}: ${d.firstCount}x em 1º | ${d.top3Count}x no TOP3 | amostras=${d.samples} | peso=${(d.weight * 100).toFixed(0)}%`
03282:         ),
03283:       ],
03284:       meta: {
03285:         trigger: {
03286:           ymd: lastY,
03287:           hour: lastH,
03288:           grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03289:         },
03290:         next: {
03291:           ymd: safeStr(targetY),
03292:           hour: safeStr(targetH),
03293:         },
03294:         samples: history.length,
03295:         period: {
03296:           from: pickDrawYMD(history[0]) || "",
03297:           to: pickDrawYMD(history[history.length - 1]) || "",
03298:         },
03299:         scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03300:         explain: {
03301:           engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03302:           targetDow,
03303:           targetDayOfMonth,
03304:           prevHour: lastH,
03305:           prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03306:           activeWeights,
03307:           scene: {
03308:             samples: Number(sceneHypothesis?.samples || 0),
03309:             totalWeight: Number(sceneHypothesis?.totalWeight || 0),
03310:             weight: Number(sceneWeight || 0),
03311:             currentSignature: currentScene?.signature || "",
03312:           },
03313:           layers: Object.fromEntries(
03314:             Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
03315:               k,
03316:               {
03317:                 label: v.label,
03318:                 samples: v.samples,
03319:                 weight: activeWeights[k],
03320:               },
03321:             ])
03322:           ),
03323:           details: x.details,
03324:         },
03325:       },
03326:     };
03327:   });
03328: 
03329:   return {
03330:     top,
03331:     meta: {
03332:       trigger: {
03333:         ymd: lastY,
03334:         hour: lastH,
03335:         grupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03336:       },
03337:       next: {
03338:         ymd: safeStr(targetY),
03339:         hour: safeStr(targetH),
03340:       },
03341:       samples: history.length,
03342:       scenario: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03343:       explain: {
03344:         engine: "V3_STATISTICAL",
03345:         targetDow,
03346:         targetDayOfMonth,
03347:         prevHour: lastH,
03348:         prevGrupo: Number(prevGrupo),
03349:         activeWeights,
03350:         layers: Object.fromEntries(
03351:           Object.entries(layers).map(([k, v]) => [
03352:             k,
03353:             {
03354:               label: v.label,
03355:               samples: v.samples,
03356:               weight: activeWeights[k],
03357:             },
03358:           ])
03359:         ),
03360:       },
03361:     },
03362:   };
03363: }
03364: 
03365: 
03366: 
03367: /* =========================
03368:    TOP3 SCENE READER
03369: ========================= */
03370: 
03371: function scenePickMilhar4(prize) {
03372:   const candidates = [
03373:     prize?.milhar,
03374:     prize?.milhar4,
03375:     prize?.numero,
03376:     prize?.number,
03377:     prize?.value,
03378:     prize?.result,
03379:     prize?.resultado,
03380:     prize?.premio,
03381:   ];
03382: 
03383:   for (const v of candidates) {
03384:     const dig = String(v ?? "").replace(/\D+/g, "");
03385:     if (dig) return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
03386:   }
03387: 
03388:   return "";
03389: }
03390: 
03391: function sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(m4) {
03392:   const s = String(m4 || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03393:   if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(s)) return null;
03394: 
03395:   const dzRaw = Number(s.slice(-2));
03396:   if (!Number.isFinite(dzRaw)) return null;
03397: 
03398:   const dz = dzRaw === 0 ? 100 : dzRaw;
03399:   const grupo = Math.ceil(dz / 4);
03400: 
03401:   return grupo >= 1 && grupo <= 25 ? grupo : null;
03402: }
03403: 
03404: function buildSceneFromDraw(draw) {
03405:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
03406: 
03407:   const rows = prizes
03408:     .map((p) => {
03409:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(p));
03410:       const milhar = scenePickMilhar4(p);
03411:       const grupo = sceneGrupoFromMilhar4(milhar);
03412: 
03413:       return {
03414:         pos,
03415:         milhar: String(milhar || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4),
03416:         centena: getCentena3(milhar),
03417:         dezena: getDezena2(milhar),
03418:         grupo,
03419:       };
03420:     })
03421:     .filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x.pos) && x.pos >= 1 && x.pos <= 7)
03422:     .sort((a, b) => a.pos - b.pos);
03423: 
03424:   const grupos = rows.map((x) => x.grupo).filter((x) => Number.isFinite(x));
03425:   const dezenas = rows.map((x) => x.dezena).filter(Boolean);
03426:   const centenas = rows.map((x) => x.centena).filter(Boolean);
03427:   const milhares = rows.map((x) => x.milhar).filter(Boolean);
03428: 
03429:   const countMap = (arr) => {
03430:     const m = new Map();
03431:     for (const x of arr) m.set(String(x), (m.get(String(x)) || 0) + 1);
03432:     return Array.from(m.entries())
03433:       .filter(([, n]) => n > 1)
03434:       .map(([value, count]) => ({ value, count }));
03435:   };
03436: 
03437:   return {
03438:     ymd: pickDrawYMD(draw),
03439:     hour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw)),
03440:     firstGrupo: grupos[0] ?? null,
03441:     grupos,
03442:     dezenas,
03443:     centenas,
03444:     milhares,
03445:     repeatedGroups: countMap(grupos),
03446:     repeatedDezenas: countMap(dezenas),
03447:     repeatedCentenas: countMap(centenas),
03448:     rows,
03449:     signature: grupos.map((g) => String(g).padStart(2, "0")).join("-"),
03450:   };
03451: }
03452: 
03453: 
03454: 
03455: function compareScenes(a, b, explain = false) {
03456:   if (!a || !b) return explain ? { score: 0, details: {} } : 0;
03457: 
03458:   let score = 0;
03459:   const details = {};
03460: 
03461:   const add = (key, value) => {
03462:     if (!value) return;
03463:     score += value;
03464:     details[key] = value;
03465:   };
03466: 
03467:   const ag = Array.isArray(a.grupos) ? a.grupos : [];
03468:   const bg = Array.isArray(b.grupos) ? b.grupos : [];
03469: 
03470:   const ad = Array.isArray(a.dezenas) ? a.dezenas : [];
03471:   const bd = Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas : [];
03472: 
03473:   const ac = Array.isArray(a.centenas) ? a.centenas : [];
03474:   const bc = Array.isArray(b.centenas) ? b.centenas : [];
03475: 
03476:   if (Number(a.firstGrupo) === Number(b.firstGrupo))
03477:     add("firstGrupo",45);
03478: 
03479:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(ag.length,bg.length,7); i++) {
03480:     if (Number(ag[i]) === Number(bg[i]))
03481:       add("order_"+i, i===0 ? 20 : 10);
03482:   }
03483: 
03484:   add(
03485:     "groupOverlap",
03486:     new Set(ag.filter(g => bg.includes(g))).size * 6
03487:   );
03488: 
03489:   add(
03490:     "dezenaOverlap",
03491:     new Set(ad.filter(d => bd.includes(d))).size * 3
03492:   );
03493: 
03494:   add(
03495:     "centenaOverlap",
03496:     new Set(ac.filter(c => bc.includes(c))).size * 1.5
03497:   );
03498: 
03499:   const repA = Array.isArray(a.repeatedGroups) ? a.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
03500:   const repB = Array.isArray(b.repeatedGroups) ? b.repeatedGroups.length : 0;
03501: 
03502:   if (repA > 0 && repB > 0)
03503:     add("repeatExists",8);
03504: 
03505:   if (repA === repB && repA > 0)
03506:     add("repeatEqual",6);
03507: 
03508:   if (String(a.signature||"") === String(b.signature||""))
03509:     add("signature",60);
03510: 
03511:   if (a.hour === b.hour)
03512:     add("hour",8);
03513: 
03514:   return explain ? { score, details } : score;
03515: }
03516: 
03517: 
03518: 
03519: 
03520: function buildHistoricalSceneRanking(draws, currentScene, limit = 100) {
03521:   const list = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
03522:   if (!currentScene) return [];
03523: 
03524:   const ordered = list
03525:     .map((draw) => ({
03526:       draw,
03527:       scene: buildSceneFromDraw(draw),
03528:     }))
03529:     .filter((x) => x.scene)
03530:     .sort((a, b) => {
03531:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
03532:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
03533:       return Number(ta || 0) - Number(tb || 0);
03534:     });
03535: 
03536:   return ordered
03537:     .map((item, idx) => {
03538:       const next = ordered[idx + 1] || null;
03539:       const score = compareScenes(currentScene, item.scene);
03540: 
03541:       return {
03542:         draw: item.draw,
03543:         scene: item.scene,
03544:         nextDraw: next?.draw || null,
03545:         nextScene: next?.scene || null,
03546:         score,
03547:       };
03548:     })
03549:     .filter((x) => x.scene && x.nextDraw && Number(x.score) > 0)
03550:     .sort((a, b) => {
03551:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) {
03552:         return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
03553:       }
03554: 
03555:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(a.scene?.ymd, a.scene?.hour);
03556:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(b.scene?.ymd, b.scene?.hour);
03557:       return Number(tb || 0) - Number(ta || 0);
03558:     })
03559:     .slice(0, Math.max(1, Number(limit || 100)));
03560: }
03561: 
03562: 
03563: 
03564: 
03565: function buildSceneHypothesisDistribution(sceneRanking, groupsK = TOP3_GROUPS_K) {
03566:   const ranking = Array.isArray(sceneRanking) ? sceneRanking : [];
03567:   const k = safeInt(groupsK, 25);
03568: 
03569:   const freq = new Map();
03570:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) freq.set(g, 0);
03571: 
03572:   let totalWeight = 0;
03573: 
03574:   for (const item of ranking) {
03575:     const score = Math.max(0, Number(item?.score || 0));
03576:     const nextGrupo = Number(item?.nextDraw ? pickPrize1GrupoFromDraw(item.nextDraw) : null);
03577: 
03578:     if (!Number.isFinite(nextGrupo) || nextGrupo < 1 || nextGrupo > k) continue;
03579:     if (score <= 0) continue;
03580: 
03581:     freq.set(nextGrupo, Number(freq.get(nextGrupo) || 0) + score);
03582:     totalWeight += score;
03583:   }
03584: 
03585:   const prob = new Map();
03586:   for (let g = 1; g <= k; g += 1) {
03587:     prob.set(g, totalWeight > 0 ? Number(freq.get(g) || 0) / totalWeight : 0);
03588:   }
03589: 
03590:   return {
03591:     freq,
03592:     prob,
03593:     samples: ranking.length,
03594:     totalWeight,
03595:   };
03596: }
03597: 
03598: 
03599: /* =========================
03600:    16/20 milhares (por grupo) — POR TERMINAÇÃO (CORRETO)
03601: ========================= */
03602: 
03603: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo2) {
03604:   const g = Number(grupo2);
03605:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
03606: 
03607:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
03608:   const out = [];
03609: 
03610:   for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
03611:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
03612:   }
03613: 
03614:   return out.filter(Boolean);
03615: }
03616: 
03617: 
03618: export function buildMilharesForGrupo({
03619:   rangeDraws,
03620:   analysisHourBucket,
03621:   schedule,
03622:   grupo2,
03623:   count = 20,
03624:   targetYmd = "",
03625: }) {
03626:   const list = Array.isArray(rangeDraws) ? rangeDraws : [];
03627:   const target = toHourBucket(analysisHourBucket);
03628:   const schSet = scheduleSet(schedule);
03629:   const targetDay = safeStr(targetYmd);
03630:   const targetDow = isYMD(targetDay) ? getDowKey(targetDay) : null;
03631: 
03632:   const N = Number.isFinite(Number(count))
03633:     ? Math.max(4, Math.trunc(Number(count)))
03634:     : 20;
03635: 
03636:   const grupoNum = Number(grupo2);
03637: 
03638:   if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum) || grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25 || !list.length) {
03639:     return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03640:   }
03641: 
03642:   const dezenasFixas = getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupoNum);
03643:   if (!dezenasFixas.length) return { dezenas: [], slots: [] };
03644: 
03645:   const perDezena = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(N / dezenasFixas.length));
03646:   const byMilhar = new Map();
03647: 
03648:   for (const d of list) {
03649:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
03650:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
03651:     if (!h) continue;
03652: 
03653:     const isTargetHour = target && h === target;
03654:     const isScheduleHour = schSet.size && schSet.has(h);
03655:     const isSameDow = targetDow !== null && Number(getDowKey(y)) === Number(targetDow);
03656:     const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
03657: 
03658:     const ps = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
03659: 
03660:     for (const prize of ps) {
03661:       const pos = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
03662:       if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 7) continue;
03663: 
03664:       const g = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
03665:       if (g !== grupoNum) continue;
03666: 
03667:       const m4 = String(pickPrizeMilhar4(prize) || "").replace(/\D+/g, "").padStart(4, "0").slice(-4);
03668:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(m4)) continue;
03669: 
03670:       const dz = getDezena2(m4);
03671:       const c3 = getCentena3(m4);
03672: 
03673:       if (!dz || !c3 || !dezenasFixas.includes(dz)) continue;
03674: 
03675:       const current = byMilhar.get(m4) || {
03676:         milhar: m4,
03677:         dezena: dz,
03678:         centena: c3,
03679:         freq: 0,
03680:         score: 0,
03681:         targetHits: 0,
03682:         scheduleHits: 0,
03683:         sameDowHits: 0,
03684:         firstPrizeHits: 0,
03685:         lastTs: 0,
03686:       };
03687: 
03688:       let inc = 1;
03689: 
03690:       if (isTargetHour) {
03691:         inc += 8;
03692:         current.targetHits += 1;
03693:       }
03694: 
03695:       if (isScheduleHour) {
03696:         inc += 3;
03697:         current.scheduleHits += 1;
03698:       }
03699: 
03700:       if (isSameDow) {
03701:         inc += 2;
03702:         current.sameDowHits += 1;
03703:       }
03704: 
03705:       if (pos === 1) {
03706:         inc += 3;
03707:         current.firstPrizeHits += 1;
03708:       } else if (pos <= 3) {
03709:         inc += 1;
03710:       }
03711: 
03712:       current.freq += 1;
03713:       current.score += inc;
03714: 
03715:       if (Number.isFinite(ts)) {
03716:         current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), ts);
03717:       }
03718: 
03719:       byMilhar.set(m4, current);
03720:     }
03721:   }
03722: 
03723:   const ranked = Array.from(byMilhar.values())
03724:     .map((m) => {
03725:       const freq = Number(m.freq || 0);
03726:       const targetHits = Number(m.targetHits || 0);
03727:       const scheduleHits = Number(m.scheduleHits || 0);
03728:       const sameDowHits = Number(m.sameDowHits || 0);
03729:       const firstPrizeHits = Number(m.firstPrizeHits || 0);
03730:       const recencyBoost = Number(m.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 1 : 0;
03731: 
03732:       return {
03733:         ...m,
03734:         score:
03735:           freq * 100 +
03736:           targetHits * 70 +
03737:           scheduleHits * 35 +
03738:           sameDowHits * 25 +
03739:           firstPrizeHits * 45 +
03740:           recencyBoost * 5,
03741:       };
03742:     })
03743:     .sort((a, b) => {
03744:       if (Number(b.score) !== Number(a.score)) return Number(b.score) - Number(a.score);
03745:       if (Number(b.targetHits) !== Number(a.targetHits)) return Number(b.targetHits) - Number(a.targetHits);
03746:       if (Number(b.freq) !== Number(a.freq)) return Number(b.freq) - Number(a.freq);
03747:       if (Number(b.lastTs) !== Number(a.lastTs)) return Number(b.lastTs) - Number(a.lastTs);
03748:       return milharCompareAsc(a.milhar, b.milhar);
03749:     });
03750: 
03751:   const centenaStats = new Map();
03752: 
03753:   for (const item of ranked) {
03754:     const centena = String(item?.centena || "").trim();
03755:     if (!centena) continue;
03756: 
03757:     const current = centenaStats.get(centena) || {
03758:       centena,
03759:       freq: 0,
03760:       score: 0,
03761:       targetHits: 0,
03762:       sameDowHits: 0,
03763:       firstPrizeHits: 0,
03764:       lastTs: 0,
03765:     };
03766: 
03767:     current.freq += Number(item.freq || 0);
03768:     current.score += Number(item.score || 0);
03769:     current.targetHits += Number(item.targetHits || 0);
03770:     current.sameDowHits += Number(item.sameDowHits || 0);
03771:     current.firstPrizeHits += Number(item.firstPrizeHits || 0);
03772:     current.lastTs = Math.max(Number(current.lastTs || 0), Number(item.lastTs || 0));
03773: 
03774:     centenaStats.set(centena, current);
03775:   }
03776: 
03777:   const centenaScoreMap = new Map();
03778: 
03779:   for (const c of centenaStats.values()) {
03780:     centenaScoreMap.set(
03781:       c.centena,
03782:       Number(c.score || 0) +
03783:         Number(c.freq || 0) * 25 +
03784:         Number(c.targetHits || 0) * 60 +
03785:         Number(c.sameDowHits || 0) * 30 +
03786:         Number(c.firstPrizeHits || 0) * 40 +
03787:         (Number(c.lastTs || 0) > 0 ? 10 : 0)
03788:     );
03789:   }
03790: 
03791:   /*
03792:    * Contexto compartilhado do seletor de milhar.
03793:    *
03794:    * O Top3 continua escolhendo:
03795:    * - grupo;
03796:    * - dezenas;
03797:    * - centenas.
03798:    *
03799:    * O chooseBestMilhar escolhe somente o prefixo vencedor
03800:    * para cada centena selecionada.
03801:    */
03802:   const fallbackMilharPrizes = [];
03803:   const primaryMilharPrizes = [];
03804: 
03805:   for (const draw of list) {
03806:     const drawYmd = pickDrawYMD(draw);
03807:     const drawHour = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
03808:     const drawDow =
03809:       isYMD(drawYmd)
03810:         ? getDowKey(drawYmd)
03811:         : null;
03812: 
03813:     const matchesTargetHour =
03814:       Boolean(target) &&
03815:       drawHour === target;
03816: 
03817:     const matchesTargetDow =
03818:       targetDow === null ||
03819:       Number(drawDow) === Number(targetDow);
03820: 
03821:     const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes)
03822:       ? draw.prizes
03823:       : [];
03824: 
03825:     for (const prize of prizes) {
03826:       const position = Number(guessPrizePos(prize));
03827:       const prizeGroup = Number(guessPrizeGrupo(prize));
03828: 
03829:       if (
03830:         !Number.isFinite(position) ||
03831:         position < 1 ||
03832:         position > 7 ||
03833:         prizeGroup !== grupoNum
03834:       ) {
03835:         continue;
03836:       }
03837: 
03838:       fallbackMilharPrizes.push(prize);
03839: 
03840:       if (matchesTargetHour && matchesTargetDow) {
03841:         primaryMilharPrizes.push(prize);
03842:       }
03843:     }
03844:   }
03845: 
03846:   const usedMilhares = new Set();
03847:   const slots = [];
03848: 
03849:   function pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, limit) {
03850:     const candidates = ranked
03851:       .filter((item) => item.dezena === dz)
03852:       .map((item) => {
03853:         const centena = String(
03854:           item?.centena || ""
03855:         )
03856:           .replace(/\D+/g, "")
03857:           .padStart(3, "0")
03858:           .slice(-3);
03859: 
03860:         return {
03861:           ...item,
03862:           centena,
03863:           centenaScore: Number(
03864:             centenaScoreMap.get(centena) || 0
03865:           ),
03866:         };
03867:       })
03868:       .filter((item) => /^\d{3}$/.test(item.centena));
03869: 
03870:     const byCentena = new Map();
03871: 
03872:     for (const item of candidates) {
03873:       const current = byCentena.get(item.centena);
03874: 
03875:       if (
03876:         !current ||
03877:         Number(item.centenaScore || 0) >
03878:           Number(current.centenaScore || 0) ||
03879:         (
03880:           Number(item.centenaScore || 0) ===
03881:             Number(current.centenaScore || 0) &&
03882:           Number(item.score || 0) >
03883:             Number(current.score || 0)
03884:         )
03885:       ) {
03886:         byCentena.set(item.centena, item);
03887:       }
03888:     }
03889: 
03890:     const strongestCentenas = Array.from(
03891:       byCentena.values()
03892:     ).sort((a, b) => {
03893:       if (
03894:         Number(b.centenaScore || 0) !==
03895:         Number(a.centenaScore || 0)
03896:       ) {
03897:         return (
03898:           Number(b.centenaScore || 0) -
03899:           Number(a.centenaScore || 0)
03900:         );
03901:       }
03902: 
03903:       if (
03904:         Number(b.score || 0) !==
03905:         Number(a.score || 0)
03906:       ) {
03907:         return (
03908:           Number(b.score || 0) -
03909:           Number(a.score || 0)
03910:         );
03911:       }
03912: 
03913:       if (
03914:         Number(b.targetHits || 0) !==
03915:         Number(a.targetHits || 0)
03916:       ) {
03917:         return (
03918:           Number(b.targetHits || 0) -
03919:           Number(a.targetHits || 0)
03920:         );
03921:       }
03922: 
03923:       if (
03924:         Number(b.freq || 0) !==
03925:         Number(a.freq || 0)
03926:       ) {
03927:         return (
03928:           Number(b.freq || 0) -
03929:           Number(a.freq || 0)
03930:         );
03931:       }
03932: 
03933:       return String(a.centena).localeCompare(
03934:         String(b.centena)
03935:       );
03936:     });
03937: 
03938:     const picked = [];
03939: 
03940:     for (const centenaItem of strongestCentenas) {
03941:       if (picked.length >= limit) break;
03942: 
03943:       const sharedResult = chooseBestMilhar({
03944:         centena: centenaItem.centena,
03945:         prizes: primaryMilharPrizes,
03946:         fallbackPrizes: fallbackMilharPrizes,
03947:       });
03948: 
03949:       const sharedWinner =
03950:         sharedResult?.winner || null;
03951: 
03952:       const selectedMilhar = String(
03953:         sharedWinner?.milhar || ""
03954:       )
03955:         .replace(/\D+/g, "")
03956:         .padStart(4, "0")
03957:         .slice(-4);
03958: 
03959:       if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(selectedMilhar)) {
03960:         continue;
03961:       }
03962: 
03963:       if (
03964:         selectedMilhar.slice(-3) !==
03965:         centenaItem.centena
03966:       ) {
03967:         continue;
03968:       }
03969: 
03970:       if (
03971:         getDezena2(selectedMilhar) !== dz ||
03972:         usedMilhares.has(selectedMilhar) ||
03973:         picked.some(
03974:           (item) =>
03975:             item.milhar === selectedMilhar
03976:         )
03977:       ) {
03978:         continue;
03979:       }
03980: 
03981:       picked.push({
03982:         ...centenaItem,
03983:         milhar: selectedMilhar,
03984:         prefix:
03985:           sharedWinner?.prefix ??
03986:           selectedMilhar.slice(0, 1),
03987:         adjustedScore: Number(
03988:           sharedWinner?.score ||
03989:           centenaItem.score ||
03990:           0
03991:         ),
03992:         sharedMilharModel:
03993:           sharedResult?.model ||
03994:           "MILHAR_PROBABILITY_V2",
03995:         sharedMilharSampleSize: Number(
03996:           sharedResult?.sampleSize || 0
03997:         ),
03998:       });
03999:     }
04000: 
04001:     return picked.slice(0, limit);
04002:   }
04003: 
04004:   for (const dz of dezenasFixas) {
04005:     const items = pickDiversifiedMilharesForDezena(dz, perDezena);
04006: 
04007:     let pushed = 0;
04008: 
04009:     for (const item of items) {
04010:       usedMilhares.add(item.milhar);
04011:       slots.push({
04012:         dezena: dz,
04013:         milhar: item.milhar,
04014:         score: item.score,
04015:         adjustedScore: item.adjustedScore,
04016:         centena: item.centena,
04017:         centenaScore: item.centenaScore,
04018:         freq: item.freq,
04019:         targetHits: item.targetHits,
04020:         scheduleHits: item.scheduleHits,
04021:         sameDowHits: item.sameDowHits,
04022:         firstPrizeHits: item.firstPrizeHits,
04023:       });
04024:       pushed += 1;
04025:     }
04026: 
04027:     while (pushed < perDezena) {
04028:       slots.push({ dezena: dz, milhar: "" });
04029:       pushed += 1;
04030:     }
04031:   }
04032: 
04033:   while (slots.length < N) slots.push({ dezena: "", milhar: "" });
04034: 
04035:   return { dezenas: dezenasFixas, slots: slots.slice(0, N) };
04036: }
04037: 
04038: export function build16MilharesForGrupo(args) {
04039:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 16 });
04040: }
04041: 
04042: export function build20MilharesForGrupo(args) {
04043:   return buildMilharesForGrupo({ ...(args || {}), count: 20 });
04044: }
04045: 
04046: /* =========================
04047:    TIMELINE DO DIA (CADEIA PREDITIVA)
04048: ========================= */
04049: 
04050: 
04051: 
04052: export function auditTop3Timeline({
04053:   timeline,
04054:   lotteryKey = "",
04055: }) {
04056:   const rows = Array.isArray(timeline) ? timeline : [];
04057: 
04058:   const validated = rows.filter((slot) => {
04059:     const status = String(slot?.status || "").toLowerCase();
04060: 
04061:     return (
04062:       status === "validated" &&
04063:       Number.isFinite(Number(slot?.resultGrupo))
04064:     );
04065:   });
04066: 
04067:   function pct(n, d) {
04068:     if (!d) return 0;
04069: 
04070:     return Number(
04071:       ((Number(n || 0) / Number(d || 1)) * 100).toFixed(2)
04072:     );
04073:   }
04074: 
04075:   function normalizeResultTop3(slot) {
04076:     const source = Array.isArray(slot?.resultTop3Groups)
04077:       ? slot.resultTop3Groups
04078:       : [];
04079: 
04080:     const normalized = source
04081:       .slice(0, 3)
04082:       .map((grupo) => {
04083:         const value = Number(grupo);
04084: 
04085:         return Number.isFinite(value) &&
04086:           value >= 1 &&
04087:           value <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04088:           ? value
04089:           : null;
04090:       });
04091: 
04092:     while (normalized.length < 3) {
04093:       normalized.push(null);
04094:     }
04095: 
04096:     if (!Number.isFinite(Number(normalized[0]))) {
04097:       const fallback = Number(slot?.resultGrupo);
04098: 
04099:       normalized[0] =
04100:         Number.isFinite(fallback) &&
04101:         fallback >= 1 &&
04102:         fallback <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04103:           ? fallback
04104:           : null;
04105:     }
04106: 
04107:     return normalized;
04108:   }
04109: 
04110:   function evaluateSlot(slot) {
04111:     const picks = (Array.isArray(slot?.top3) ? slot.top3 : [])
04112:       .slice(0, 3)
04113:       .map((item) => Number(item?.grupo))
04114:       .filter(
04115:         (grupo) =>
04116:           Number.isFinite(grupo) &&
04117:           grupo >= 1 &&
04118:           grupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04119:       );
04120: 
04121:     const resultTop3 = normalizeResultTop3(slot);
04122:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3[0]);
04123: 
04124:     const top1Hit =
04125:       picks.length > 0 &&
04126:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
04127:       Number(picks[0]) === resultGrupo;
04128: 
04129:     const top3Hit =
04130:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
04131:       picks.some(
04132:         (grupo) => Number(grupo) === resultGrupo
04133:       );
04134: 
04135:     const prizePositionHits = resultTop3.map(
04136:       (grupo) =>
04137:         Number.isFinite(Number(grupo)) &&
04138:         picks.some(
04139:           (pick) => Number(pick) === Number(grupo)
04140:         )
04141:     );
04142: 
04143:     const predictionHits = picks.map((grupo) =>
04144:       resultTop3.some(
04145:         (result) =>
04146:           Number.isFinite(Number(result)) &&
04147:           Number(result) === Number(grupo)
04148:       )
04149:     );
04150: 
04151:     const matchedPrizePositions =
04152:       prizePositionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
04153: 
04154:     const matchedPredictions =
04155:       predictionHits.filter(Boolean).length;
04156: 
04157:     return {
04158:       picks,
04159:       resultTop3,
04160:       resultGrupo,
04161: 
04162:       top1Hit,
04163:       top3Hit,
04164: 
04165:       prize1Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[0]),
04166:       prize2Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[1]),
04167:       prize3Hit: Boolean(prizePositionHits[2]),
04168: 
04169:       top3PrizeHit: matchedPrizePositions > 0,
04170: 
04171:       matchedPrizePositions,
04172:       matchedPredictions,
04173: 
04174:       prizePositionHits,
04175:       predictionHits,
04176:     };
04177:   }
04178: 
04179:   const evaluated = validated.map((slot) => ({
04180:     slot,
04181:     metrics: evaluateSlot(slot),
04182:   }));
04183: 
04184:   function summarize(items) {
04185:     const summary = {
04186:       total: items.length,
04187: 
04188:       top1Hits: 0,
04189:       top3Hits: 0,
04190: 
04191:       prize1Hits: 0,
04192:       prize2Hits: 0,
04193:       prize3Hits: 0,
04194: 
04195:       top3PrizeHits: 0,
04196: 
04197:       matchedPrizePositions: 0,
04198:       matchedPredictions: 0,
04199:     };
04200: 
04201:     for (const item of items) {
04202:       const metrics = item.metrics;
04203: 
04204:       if (metrics.top1Hit) summary.top1Hits += 1;
04205:       if (metrics.top3Hit) summary.top3Hits += 1;
04206: 
04207:       if (metrics.prize1Hit) summary.prize1Hits += 1;
04208:       if (metrics.prize2Hit) summary.prize2Hits += 1;
04209:       if (metrics.prize3Hit) summary.prize3Hits += 1;
04210: 
04211:       if (metrics.top3PrizeHit) {
04212:         summary.top3PrizeHits += 1;
04213:       }
04214: 
04215:       summary.matchedPrizePositions +=
04216:         Number(metrics.matchedPrizePositions || 0);
04217: 
04218:       summary.matchedPredictions +=
04219:         Number(metrics.matchedPredictions || 0);
04220:     }
04221: 
04222:     return {
04223:       ...summary,
04224: 
04225:       top1Rate: pct(summary.top1Hits, summary.total),
04226:       top3Rate: pct(summary.top3Hits, summary.total),
04227: 
04228:       prize1Rate: pct(summary.prize1Hits, summary.total),
04229:       prize2Rate: pct(summary.prize2Hits, summary.total),
04230:       prize3Rate: pct(summary.prize3Hits, summary.total),
04231: 
04232:       top3PrizeRate: pct(
04233:         summary.top3PrizeHits,
04234:         summary.total
04235:       ),
04236: 
04237:       averageMatchedPrizePositions: summary.total
04238:         ? Number(
04239:             (
04240:               summary.matchedPrizePositions /
04241:               summary.total
04242:             ).toFixed(4)
04243:           )
04244:         : 0,
04245: 
04246:       averageMatchedPredictions: summary.total
04247:         ? Number(
04248:             (
04249:               summary.matchedPredictions /
04250:               summary.total
04251:             ).toFixed(4)
04252:           )
04253:         : 0,
04254:     };
04255:   }
04256: 
04257:   function groupBy(keyFn) {
04258:     const map = new Map();
04259: 
04260:     for (const item of evaluated) {
04261:       const key = keyFn(item.slot);
04262: 
04263:       if (!map.has(key)) {
04264:         map.set(key, []);
04265:       }
04266: 
04267:       map.get(key).push(item);
04268:     }
04269: 
04270:     return Array.from(map.entries())
04271:       .map(([key, items]) => ({
04272:         key,
04273:         ...summarize(items),
04274:       }))
04275:       .sort((a, b) => {
04276:         if (b.top3PrizeRate !== a.top3PrizeRate) {
04277:           return b.top3PrizeRate - a.top3PrizeRate;
04278:         }
04279: 
04280:         if (b.top3Rate !== a.top3Rate) {
04281:           return b.top3Rate - a.top3Rate;
04282:         }
04283: 
04284:         return b.total - a.total;
04285:       });
04286:   }
04287: 
04288:   const summary = summarize(evaluated);
04289: 
04290:   return {
04291:     lotteryKey,
04292:     ...summary,
04293: 
04294:     byHour: groupBy(
04295:       (slot) => String(slot?.targetHour || "")
04296:     ),
04297: 
04298:     byDate: groupBy(
04299:       (slot) => String(slot?.targetYmd || "")
04300:     ),
04301: 
04302:     rows: evaluated.map(({ slot, metrics }) => ({
04303:       ymd: slot.targetYmd,
04304:       hour: slot.targetHour,
04305: 
04306:       baseYmd: slot.baseYmd,
04307:       baseHour: slot.baseHour,
04308: 
04309:       resultGrupo: metrics.resultGrupo,
04310:       resultTop3: metrics.resultTop3,
04311: 
04312:       top3: metrics.picks,
04313: 
04314:       top1Hit: metrics.top1Hit,
04315:       top3Hit: metrics.top3Hit,
04316: 
04317:       prize1Hit: metrics.prize1Hit,
04318:       prize2Hit: metrics.prize2Hit,
04319:       prize3Hit: metrics.prize3Hit,
04320: 
04321:       top3PrizeHit: metrics.top3PrizeHit,
04322: 
04323:       matchedPrizePositions:
04324:         metrics.matchedPrizePositions,
04325: 
04326:       matchedPredictions:
04327:         metrics.matchedPredictions,
04328: 
04329:       prizePositionHits:
04330:         metrics.prizePositionHits,
04331: 
04332:       predictionHits:
04333:         metrics.predictionHits,
04334: 
04335:       historyStats: slot.historyStats || null,
04336:     })),
04337:   };
04338: }
