04341: function buildTimelineForDate({
04342:   ymd,
04343:   drawsToday,
04344:   drawsRange,
04345:   lotteryKey,
04346:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04347:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04348:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04349: }) {
04350:   const targetYmd = safeStr(ymd);
04351:   const day = Array.isArray(drawsToday) ? [...drawsToday] : [];
04352:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? [...drawsRange] : [];
04353: 
04354:   if (!isYMD(targetYmd) || !range.length) return [];
04355: 
04356:   const schedule = getScheduleForLottery({
04357:     lotteryKey,
04358:     ymd: targetYmd,
04359:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04360:     PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04361:     FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04362:   });
04363: 
04364:   const normalizedSchedule = (Array.isArray(schedule) ? schedule : [])
04365:     .map(toHourBucket)
04366:     .filter(Boolean);
04367: 
04368:   if (!normalizedSchedule.length) return [];
04369: 
04370:   const daySorted = day
04371:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
04372:     .sort((a, b) => {
04373:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04374:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04375:       return ta - tb;
04376:     });
04377: 
04378:   const rangeSorted = range
04379:     .filter((d) => isYMD(pickDrawYMD(d)) && toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d)))
04380:     .sort((a, b) => {
04381:       const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04382:       const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04383:       return ta - tb;
04384:     });
04385: 
04386:   const realizedTodayMap = new Map();
04387: 
04388:   for (const d of daySorted) {
04389:     const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04390:     const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04391:     if (!isYMD(y) || !h) continue;
04392:     if (y !== targetYmd) continue;
04393:     realizedTodayMap.set(`${y}|${h}`, d);
04394:   }
04395: 
04396: 
04397:   const chainContextDraws = [];
04398:   const timeline = [];
04399:   let pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = false;
04400: 
04401:   for (const slotHour of normalizedSchedule) {
04402:     const currentDraw = realizedTodayMap.get(`${targetYmd}|${slotHour}`) || null;
04403:     const slotTs = ymdHourToTs(targetYmd, slotHour);
04404: 
04405: 
04406:     if (!currentDraw && pendingPredictionAlreadyShown) {
04407:       timeline.push({
04408:         targetYmd,
04409:         targetHour: slotHour,
04410:         baseYmd: "",
04411:         baseHour: "",
04412:         top3: [],
04413:         resultGrupo: null,
04414:         hit: null,
04415:         status: "pending",
04416:       });
04417:       continue;
04418:     }
04419: 
04420:     let baseDraw = null;
04421: 
04422:     const priorChainDraw = [...chainContextDraws]
04423:       .filter((d) => {
04424:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04425:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04426:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04427:         return isYMD(y) && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
04428:       })
04429:       .sort((a, b) => {
04430:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04431:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04432:         return tb - ta;
04433:       })[0] || null;
04434: 
04435:     if (priorChainDraw) baseDraw = priorChainDraw;
04436: 
04437:     if (!baseDraw) {
04438:       const prevSameDay = daySorted
04439:         .filter((d) => {
04440:           const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04441:           const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04442:           const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04443:           return y === targetYmd && h && Number.isFinite(ts) && ts < slotTs;
04444:         })
04445:         .sort((a, b) => {
04446:           const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04447:           const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04448:           return tb - ta;
04449:         })[0] || null;
04450: 
04451:       if (prevSameDay) baseDraw = prevSameDay;
04452:     }
04453: 
04454:     if (!baseDraw) {
04455:       for (let i = rangeSorted.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
04456:         const d = rangeSorted[i];
04457:         const y = pickDrawYMD(d);
04458:         const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d));
04459:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(y, h);
04460: 
04461:         if (!Number.isFinite(ts) || ts >= slotTs) continue;
04462: 
04463:         baseDraw = d;
04464:         break;
04465:       }
04466:     }
04467: 
04468:     if (!baseDraw) {
04469:       baseDraw = findPreviousValidDraw(rangeSorted, targetYmd, slotHour);
04470:     }
04471: 
04472:     if (!baseDraw) {
04473:       timeline.push({
04474:         targetYmd,
04475:         targetHour: slotHour,
04476:         baseYmd: "",
04477:         baseHour: "",
04478:         top3: [],
04479:         resultGrupo: null,
04480:         hit: null,
04481:         status: "pending",
04482:       });
04483:       continue;
04484:     }
04485: 
04486:     const baseTs = ymdHourToTs(
04487:       pickDrawYMD(baseDraw),
04488:       toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw))
04489:     );
04490: 
04491:     const usableHistory = rangeSorted.filter((d) => {
04492:       const ts = ymdHourToTs(
04493:         pickDrawYMD(d),
04494:         toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
04495:       );
04496:       return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
04497:     });
04498: 
04499:     const historyStats = (() => {
04500:       const MS_DAY = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
04501: 
04502:       let d30 = 0;
04503:       let d90 = 0;
04504:       let d180 = 0;
04505:       let d365 = 0;
04506: 
04507:       for (const historicalDraw of usableHistory) {
04508:         const historicalTs = ymdHourToTs(
04509:           pickDrawYMD(historicalDraw),
04510:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(historicalDraw))
04511:         );
04512: 
04513:         if (!Number.isFinite(historicalTs)) continue;
04514: 
04515:         const diffDays = (baseTs - historicalTs) / MS_DAY;
04516: 
04517:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 30) d30 += 1;
04518:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 90) d90 += 1;
04519:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 180) d180 += 1;
04520:         if (diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 365) d365 += 1;
04521:       }
04522: 
04523:       return {
04524:         total: usableHistory.length,
04525:         d30,
04526:         d90,
04527:         d180,
04528:         d365,
04529:       };
04530:     })();
04531: 
04532:     const usableTodayContext = [...chainContextDraws]
04533:       .filter((d) => {
04534:         const ts = ymdHourToTs(
04535:           pickDrawYMD(d),
04536:           toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(d))
04537:         );
04538:         return Number.isFinite(ts) && ts <= baseTs;
04539:       })
04540:       .sort((a, b) => {
04541:         const ta = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(a), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(a)));
04542:         const tb = ymdHourToTs(pickDrawYMD(b), toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(b)));
04543:         return ta - tb;
04544:       });
04545: 
04546:     const computed = computeStatisticalTop3V3({
04547:       lotteryKey,
04548:       drawsRange: [...usableHistory, ...usableTodayContext],
04549:       drawLast: baseDraw,
04550:       drawsToday: usableTodayContext,
04551:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04552:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04553:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04554:       topN: 3,
04555:       targetYmdOverride: targetYmd,
04556:       targetHourOverride: slotHour,
04557:     });
04558: 
04559: 
04560:     const computedNextYmd = safeStr(computed?.meta?.next?.ymd || "");
04561:     const computedNextHour = toHourBucket(computed?.meta?.next?.hour || "");
04562:     const computedForSlot =
04563:       computedNextYmd === targetYmd && computedNextHour === slotHour;
04564: 
04565:     const top3 =
04566:       computedForSlot && Array.isArray(computed?.top)
04567:         ? computed.top.slice(0, 3)
04568:         : [];
04569: 
04570:     const resultTop3Groups = currentDraw
04571:       ? getPrizeGroupsByPosition(currentDraw, 3)
04572:       : [null, null, null];
04573: 
04574:     const resultGrupo = Number(resultTop3Groups[0]);
04575: 
04576:     const normalizedResultGrupo =
04577:       Number.isFinite(resultGrupo) &&
04578:       resultGrupo >= 1 &&
04579:       resultGrupo <= TOP3_GROUPS_K
04580:         ? resultGrupo
04581:         : null;
04582: 
04583:     const hitPrizePositions = resultTop3Groups.map(
04584:       (resultGroup) =>
04585:         Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup)) &&
04586:         top3.some(
04587:           (item) =>
04588:             Number(item?.grupo) ===
04589:             Number(resultGroup)
04590:         )
04591:     );
04592: 
04593:     const matchedPrizePositions =
04594:       hitPrizePositions.filter(Boolean).length;
04595: 
04596:     const hit =
04597:       top3.length > 0 &&
04598:       resultTop3Groups.some(
04599:         (resultGroup) =>
04600:           Number.isFinite(Number(resultGroup))
04601:       )
04602:         ? matchedPrizePositions > 0
04603:         : null;
04604: 
04605:     timeline.push({
04606:       targetYmd,
04607:       targetHour: slotHour,
04608: 
04609:       baseYmd: pickDrawYMD(baseDraw) || "",
04610:       baseHour: toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(baseDraw)) || "",
04611: 
04612:       top3,
04613: 
04614:       resultGrupo: normalizedResultGrupo,
04615:       resultTop3Groups,
04616: 
04617:       hit,
04618:       top3PrizeHit: hit,
04619:       hitPrizePositions,
04620:       matchedPrizePositions,
04621: 
04622:       status: Number.isFinite(
04623:         Number(normalizedResultGrupo)
04624:       )
04625:         ? "validated"
04626:         : "pending",
04627: 
04628:       historyStats,
04629:     });
04630: 
04631:     if (currentDraw) {
04632:       chainContextDraws.push(currentDraw);
04633:     } else {
04634:       pendingPredictionAlreadyShown = true;
04635:     }
04636:   }
04637: 
04638:   return timeline;
04639: 
04640: }
04641: 
04642: export function buildTimelineTop3(args) {
04643:   return buildTimelineForDate(args || {});
04644: }
04645: 
04646: export function auditTop3Backtest({
04647:   drawsRange,
04648:   lotteryKey,
04649:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04650:   PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04651:   FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04652: }) {
04653:   const range = Array.isArray(drawsRange) ? drawsRange : [];
04654:   const dates = Array.from(
04655:     new Set(
04656:       range
04657:         .map((d) => pickDrawYMD(d))
04658:         .filter((ymd) => isYMD(ymd))
04659:     )
04660:   ).sort();
04661: 
04662:   const allTimeline = [];
04663: 
04664:   for (const ymd of dates) {
04665:     const drawsToday = range.filter((d) => pickDrawYMD(d) === ymd);
04666: 
04667:     const dayTimeline = buildTimelineForDate({
04668:       ymd,
04669:       drawsToday,
04670:       drawsRange: range,
04671:       lotteryKey,
04672:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_NORMAL,
04673:       PT_RIO_SCHEDULE_WED_SAT,
04674:       FEDERAL_SCHEDULE,
04675:     });
04676: 
04677:     allTimeline.push(...(Array.isArray(dayTimeline) ? dayTimeline : []));
04678:   }
04679: 
04680:   return auditTop3Timeline({
04681:     timeline: allTimeline,
04682:     lotteryKey,
04683:   });
04684: }

00005:  * Score Engine V2 inicial
00006:  *
00007:  * Consome evidências.
00008:  * Não aplica regra cega isolada.

00011: function normalizeEvidenceValue(value) {
00012:   const n = Number(value);
00013:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return 0;
00014:   return n;
00015: }
00016: 
00017: function calculateEvidenceStrength(evidenceList = []) {
00018:   const list = Array.isArray(evidenceList) ? evidenceList : [];
00019: 
00020:   if (!list.length) {
00021:     return {
00022:       score: 0,
00023:       confidence: 0,
00024:       reasons: ["Sem evidências suficientes."],
00025:       signals: {},
00026:     };
00027:   }
00028: 
00029:   const values = list.map((e) => normalizeEvidenceValue(e.value));
00030:   const total = values.reduce((acc, v) => acc + v, 0);
00031:   const max = Math.max(...values, 1);
00032: 
00033:   const score = Math.min(100, Math.round((total / max) * 50));
00034:   const confidence = Math.min(100, Math.round(score * Math.min(1, list.length / 3)));
00035: 
00036:   const reasons = list.flatMap((e) => Array.isArray(e.reasons) ? e.reasons : []);
00037: 
00038:   const signals = {};
00039:   for (const e of list) {
00040:     signals[e.module] = e.evidence || {};
00041:   }
00042: 
00043:   return {
00044:     score,
00045:     confidence,
00046:     reasons,
00047:     signals,
00048:   };
00049: }
00050: 
00051: function scoreItem(item = {}, context = {}) {
00052:   const collected = collectEvidence({
00053:     item,
00054:     context,
00055:     config: scoreConfig,
00056:   });
00057: 
00058:   const strength = calculateEvidenceStrength(collected.evidence);
00059: 
00060:   const probability = Math.max(
00061:     0,
00062:     Math.min(
00063:       100,
00064:       Number(item.scoreProb || item.rawScoreProb || 0) * 100
00065:     )
00066:   );
00067: 
00068:   const finalScore = Math.round(
00069:     (strength.score * 0.45) +
00070:     (probability * 0.55)
00071:   );
00072: 
00073:   const finalConfidence = Math.round(
00074:     (strength.confidence * 0.40) +
00075:     (probability * 0.60)
00076:   );
00077: 
00078:   return {
00079:     ...item,
00080: 
00081:     score: finalScore,
00082: 
00083:     confidence: finalConfidence,
00084: 
00085:     reasons: [
00086:       ...strength.reasons,
00087:       `Probabilidade V3: ${probability.toFixed(2)}%`,
00088:     ],
00089: 
00090:     signals: {
00091:       ...strength.signals,
00092:       probability,
00093:     },
00094: 
00095:     evidenceCount: collected.count,
00096: 
00097:     evidenceModules: collected.modules,
00098:   };
00099: }
00100: 
00101: function scoreRanking(items = [], context = {}) {
00102: 
00103:   return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])
00104:     .map(item => scoreItem(item, context))
00105:     .sort((a, b) => {
00106: 
00107:       if (b.score !== a.score)
00108:         return b.score - a.score;
00109: 
00110:       if (b.confidence !== a.confidence)
00111:         return b.confidence - a.confidence;
00112: 
00113:       return (Number(b.scoreProb || 0) - Number(a.scoreProb || 0));
00114: 
00115:     });
00116: 
00117: }
00118: 
00119: export {
00120: scoreItem,
00121:   scoreRanking,
00122:   calculateEvidenceStrength,
00123: };

ARQUIVO COMPLETO: C:\Users\glaub\palpitaco\src\pages\Top3\modules\scoreEngine\evidenceEngine.js
========================================================================================================================
00001: import { buildFrequencyEvidence } from "./frequencyEvidence";
00002: import { buildContextEvidence } from "./contextEvidence";
00003: 
00004: /**
00005:  * Evidence Engine
00006:  *
00007:  * Centraliza todos os módulos de evidência.
00008:  * Cada módulo produz fatos; nenhum decide o resultado.
00009:  */
00010: 
00011: const MODULES = {
00012:   frequency: buildFrequencyEvidence,
00013:   context: buildContextEvidence,
00014: };
00015: 
00016: function collectEvidence({
00017:   item = {},
00018:   context = {},
00019:   config = {},
00020: } = {}) {
00021: 
00022:   const evidence = [];
00023:   const enabled = config.evidenceModules || {};
00024: 
00025:   for (const [name, builder] of Object.entries(MODULES)) {
00026: 
00027:     if (!enabled[name]?.enabled) {
00028:       continue;
00029:     }
00030: 
00031:     try {
00032: 
00033:       const result = builder(item, context);
00034: 
00035:       if (result) {
00036:         evidence.push(result);
00037:       }
00038: 
00039:     } catch (err) {
00040: 
00041:       evidence.push({
00042:         module: name,
00043:         error: true,
00044:         message: err.message,
00045:       });
00046: 
00047:     }
00048: 
00049:   }
00050: 
00051:   return {
00052: 
00053:     count: evidence.filter(e => !e.error).length,
00054: 
00055:     modules: evidence
00056:       .filter(e => !e.error)
00057:       .map(e => e.module),
00058: 
00059:     evidence,
00060: 
00061:   };
00062: 
00063: }
00064: 
00065: export {
00066: collectEvidence,
00067: };

00001: /**

ARQUIVO COMPLETO: C:\Users\glaub\palpitaco\src\pages\Top3\modules\scoreEngine\frequencyEvidence.js

00001: /**
00002:  * Frequency Evidence V2
00003:  *
00004:  * Responsabilidade:
00005:  * Produzir evidências relacionadas à frequência.
00006:  * Não calcula score.
00007:  */
00008: 
00009: function normalize(value) {
00010:   const n = Number(value);
00011: 
00012:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) {
00013:     return 0;
00014:   }
00015: 
00016:   return n;
00017: }
00018: 
00019: function normalizePercent(value) {
00020:   const n = Number(value);
00021: 
00022:   if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < 0) {
00023:     return 0;
00024:   }
00025: 
00026:   return Math.min(100, n);
00027: }
00028: 
00029: function classifyFrequency(percent) {
00030:   if (percent >= 70) return "very_high";
00031:   if (percent >= 50) return "high";
00032:   if (percent >= 30) return "medium";
00033:   if (percent >= 15) return "low";
00034:   return "very_low";
00035: }
00036: 
00037: function buildReasons(frequency, percent, level) {
00038:   const reasons = [];
00039: 
00040:   reasons.push(`Frequência histórica: ${frequency}`);
00041: 
00042:   if (percent > 0) {
00043:     reasons.push(`Participação: ${percent.toFixed(1)}%`);
00044:   }
00045: 
00046:   switch (level) {
00047:     case "very_high":
00048:       reasons.push("Frequência muito elevada.");
00049:       break;
00050: 
00051:     case "high":
00052:       reasons.push("Frequência elevada.");
00053:       break;
00054: 
00055:     case "medium":
00056:       reasons.push("Frequência intermediária.");
00057:       break;
00058: 
00059:     case "low":
00060:       reasons.push("Frequência baixa.");
00061:       break;
00062: 
00063:     default:
00064:       reasons.push("Pouca evidência de frequência.");
00065:   }
00066: 
00067:   return reasons;
00068: }
00069: 
00070: function buildFrequencyEvidence(item = {}, context = {}) {
00071:   const frequency = normalize(
00072:     item.frequency ??
00073:     item.total ??
00074:     item.count ??
00075:     item.appearances
00076:   );
00077: 
00078:   const totalUniverse = normalize(
00079:     context.totalDraws ??
00080:     context.windowSize ??
00081:     context.sampleSize
00082:   );
00083: 
00084:   const percent =
00085:     totalUniverse > 0
00086:       ? normalizePercent((frequency / totalUniverse) * 100)
00087:       : normalizePercent(
00088:           item.frequencyPercent ??
00089:           item.frequencyRate
00090:         );
00091: 
00092:   const level = classifyFrequency(percent);
00093: 
00094:   return {
00095:     module: "frequency",
00096: 
00097:     value: percent,
00098: 
00099:     evidence: {
00100:       frequency,
00101:       percent,
00102:       level,
00103:       totalUniverse,
00104: 
00105:       window: context.window || null,
00106:       lotteryKey: context.lotteryKey || null,
00107:       position: context.position || null,
00108:       weekday: context.weekday || null,
00109:       hour: context.hour || null,
00110:     },
00111: 
00112:     reasons: buildReasons(frequency, percent, level),
00113:   };
00114: }
00115: 
00116: export {
00117: buildFrequencyEvidence,
00118: };
